# Skills · M · part 65

822 entries, mgf-spectra-file-parsing to microbe-compound-identification. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **mgf-spectra-file-parsing**: Use when an NPLinker-based integrative omics workflow needs MS/MS spectra read from a GNPS molecular networking MGF file, e.g. as the input for CANOPUS class prediction on spectra below 850 Da. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectra-file-parsing))
- **mgf-spectra-fragment-filtering**: Use when assembling experimental MS/MS benchmarking sets from GNPS or third-party .mgf spectral libraries, and noisy, oversized, or adduct-mixed spectra must be normalized before annotation-tool evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectra-fragment-filtering))
- **mgf-spectra-handling**: Use when MS2 peak data lives as a Python object (e.g., a matchms.Spectrum loaded from MGF) but the analysis runs in R with Spectra, and you need lazy, on-demand access to peaks without duplicating data across languages. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectra-handling))
- **mgf-spectra-import**: Use when MS2 spectra were produced by an external feature-based tool (MZmine, MS-DIAL) exported as MGF/MSP files and must be loaded into R as a Spectra object for downstream matchSpectra() annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectra-import))
- **mgf-spectra-noise-filtering-and-preprocessing**: Use when raw MGF tandem MS/MS spectra contain low-abundance noise peaks or crowded m/z regions and need deterministic cleaning (intensity and m/z cutoffs, 3-Da de-clustering) before pair-based spectral similarity modeling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectra-noise-filtering-and-preprocessing))
- **mgf-spectra-parsing**: Use when you have MGF peak lists of tandem MS2 spectra (e.g. from ProteomeXchange PXD034932) and need pairwise spectral similarities or the compareMS2 set-level distance S/D between datasets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectra-parsing))
- **mgf-spectra-reading**: Use when the input is an MGF peak list file (e.g. iPRG2012.mgf) whose spectra must be loaded into Python for downstream processing or benchmarking with spectrum_utils, pymzML, or pyOpenMS. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectra-reading))
- **mgf-spectral-data-handling**: Use when input spectra are provided as MGF files (e.g., experimental metabolomics runs or reference libraries) and must be converted into matchms spectrum objects for library-search scoring, filtering, or annotation counting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectral-data-handling))
- **mgf-spectral-data-import**: Use when raw mass spectra in .mgf (or mzML/mzXML) format must be loaded and converted into a feature table before correlation-based adduct/in-source fragment detection with DBDIpy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectral-data-import--7f98239f2a475976))
- **mgf-spectral-data-import**: Use when the input is a .mgf file of MS/MS feature spectra (unique feature identifier, precursor m/z, m/z–intensity tuples) that must be loaded into Python and preprocessed before specXplore similarity or dashboard work. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectral-data-import--dff32ae14fa2c1ab))
- **mgf-spectral-file-preparation**: Use when you have ms1-id-derived pseudo MS/MS spectra from full-scan LC-MS (e.g. HILIC-positive mzML from Metabolomics Workbench PR000639) that must be reformatted into a cleaned MGF for GNPS2 molecular networking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectral-file-preparation))
- **mgf-spectral-file-processing**: Use when HR-MS/MS inference spectra or spectral libraries are supplied as MGF files and must be loaded, SMARTS-filtered into 'relevant'/'other' sets, or exported before classifier training/prediction in AnnoMe. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectral-file-processing))
- **mgf-spectrum-batch-search**: Use when you have a single .mgf file of consensus MS/MS spectra (e.g., from MZmine molecular networking) and need to determine which spectra match microbial metabolomes in GNPS/MassIVE-indexed data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectrum-batch-search))
- **mgf-spectrum-block-segmentation**: Use when the input is a text-format MGF (Mascot Generic Format) peak list file that must be segmented into individual spectra with parameter metadata and (m/z, intensity) peak lists, e.g. for downstream mzIdentML-referenced spectrum lookups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/mgf-spectrum-block-segmentation))
- **mgf-spectrum-field-parsing**: Use when input is an MGF tandem mass spectrometry file whose spectra must be parsed into FIDDLE's required 'precursor_mz' and 'precursor_type' fields before candidate formula generation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectrum-field-parsing))
- **mgf-spectrum-file-formatting**: Use when pseudo MS/MS spectra have been generated from MS1-only LC-MS or MS imaging data and need to be written as MGF entries containing precursor feature m/z, peaks, and retention time/pixel metadata for annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectrum-file-formatting))
- **mgf-spectrum-format-generation**: Use when raw vendor MS/MS files (e.g. from PRIDE or MassIVE antibody data sets) must be reformatted into an open, text-based spectral format as input for de novo peptide sequencing or database-search tools. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectrum-format-generation))
- **mgf-spectrum-import**: Use when a workflow starts from an MGF-formatted MS/MS file (e.g. all_your_spectrums.mgf or tests/testdata/pesticides.mgf) and spectra must be loaded into matchms Spectrum objects before applying default_filters or process_peaks pipelines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectrum-import))
- **mgf-spectrum-parsing**: Use when input is an MGF-formatted MS/MS spectrum file (e.g. pesticides.mgf or all_your_spectrums.mgf) and the goal is to load it into matchms Spectrum objects for downstream filtering or scoring. - **mgf-spectrum-parsing**: Use when deconvoluted GC-MS compound spectra are supplied as .mgf (from MSHub auto-deconvolution or another tool) and need to be parsed and annotated against GNPS GC-MS library standards. - **mgf-spectrum-parsing**: Use when clustering large-scale MS/MS data where raw MGF files must be converted into numeric peak arrays and preprocessed, since peak information occupies over 95% of raw-file data volume and preprocessing is a critical bottleneck contributing 20-90% of overall runtime. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mgf-spectrum-parsing--c2233cc5c4f3b90d))
- **mgf-spectrum-parsing**: Use when tandem MS/MS data are provided as MGF peak lists (e.g. from a ProteomeXchange/PRIDE deposit such as PXD034932) and spectra must be read with precursor m/z for direct spectral comparison or parameter estimation. - **micro-submission**: Use when auditing a MICRO submission before HotCRP upload — the 11-page-plus-references limit with no appendix allowed, EDT (not AoE) deadline arithmetic, the abstract-registration gate a week before the paper, minimum 9pt fonts, mandatory page numbers, all-author reference entries, and MICRO's visual-plus-automated format inspection. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/MICRO-Skills/skills/micro-submission) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **micro-supplementary**: Use when deciding where a MICRO paper's supporting material lives, because MICRO allows no appendix at all — triaging content into the 11 reviewed pages, the unlimited references, an anonymized artifact repository, or the post-acceptance two-page artifact appendix, and cutting what fits nowhere. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/MICRO-Skills/skills/micro-supplementary) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **micro-survey-optimization**: Audit and optimize on-site micro-surveys (intent polls, exit surveys, and feedback widgets) to maximize response rates and capture high-quality qualitative data that drives conversion experiments. ([coastdigitalgroup/coastai-skills](https://github.com/coastdigitalgroup/coastai-skills/tree/HEAD/website-growth/micro-survey-optimization) · ★2)
- **micro-task-loop**: Straight-through phased execution for simple, low-risk plans with few moving parts. Runs every phase back to back with no per-phase verification, builds, or commits — unless an existing plan or task list names its own check/test/commit cadence, which is then followed as written; tests, lints, and a single commit happen once at the end. Agents MUST continue through all phases autonomously without stopping between phases. Only load if explicitly asked. ([jayson-lennon/jinn](https://github.com/jayson-lennon/jinn/tree/HEAD/res/skills/micro-task-loop) · ★4)
- **micro-task-planning**: Turns a confirmed design into an ordered list of small, independently-verifiable "micro-tasks" — each with one acceptance criterion and the exact command that proves it, scaled by project Tier — so nothing moves from design to code without a clear, checkable plan. Use whenever `architect` finishes a design, and whenever `project-lead`/`builder`/`tester` need a task's exact acceptance criterion, verification command, or execution order. ([GRU953/GRU953-Studio](https://github.com/GRU953/GRU953-Studio/tree/HEAD/plugins/gru953-studio/skills/micro-task-planning) · ★1)
- **micro-tdd**: Routes each code change to its cheapest safe path (direct edit, visual check, or test-first RED→GREEN→REFACTOR); localizes unclear bugs before fixing. Use for any code change or bug fix; 3d delegates its loop here. ([PatN-git/Stratosphere-OS](https://github.com/PatN-git/Stratosphere-OS/tree/HEAD/dist/skills/micro-tdd) · 2 copies)
- **micro-topic-selection**: Use when deciding whether a project belongs at MICRO (IEEE/ACM International Symposium on Microarchitecture) or should route to ISCA, HPCA, ASPLOS, DAC/ICCAD, SC, or MLSys — testing whether the mechanism truly lives inside the machine, gauging the MICRO evidence bar, and weighing the main track against the new Industry Track. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/MICRO-Skills/skills/micro-topic-selection) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **micro-transitions**: Bouwen van micro-interactions, hover/press/enter/exit transities, project- of feature-kaarten (arc/fan/carousel/stagger) en swipe-rails, in React+Motion of vanilla HTML/CSS/JS ([robinbril/claude-harness-public](https://github.com/robinbril/claude-harness-public/tree/HEAD/skills/micro-transitions))

- **micro-workflow**: Use when planning a MICRO campaign end to end — the April-to-Athens 2026 cycle clock, the four-venue architecture calendar (MICRO/ISCA/HPCA/ASPLOS) seen from MICRO's October seat, resubmission lattices after each outcome, and starting the MICRO 2027 evaluation stack early enough to matter. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/MICRO-Skills/skills/micro-workflow) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **micro-world**: Build a throwaway interactive HTML simulation of one behavior (a state machine, an algorithm, a data transform, a protocol flow). The reader learns the behavior by operating it. ([chanmuzi/git-claw](https://github.com/chanmuzi/git-claw/tree/HEAD/skills/micro-world) · ★7)
- **micro-world**: Generate a single-file, human-DRIVEN interactive model of a subsystem or a diff — a scrubber / step-inject-rewind / before-after playground that lets a person build intuition by poking at the behavior, not just reading it. A re-implementation for understanding (not a wrapper on the live app), SHA-stamped with an honest faithful-vs-reimplemented map and staleness detection. Use when the user says "make a micro-world / playground / interactive model / simulator for &lt;subsystem&gt;", "let me play with how X works", or invokes /micro-world. Portable; touches only .understanding/. ([advatar/understand](https://github.com/advatar/understand/tree/HEAD/skills/micro-world))
- **micro-writing-style**: Use when drafting or revising a MICRO paper's prose and structure — building the characterization-motivated introduction, budgeting 11 appendix-free pages, drawing the mechanism walkthrough a microarchitect can re-implement, and pairing every speedup claim with its area, storage, and power cost line. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/MICRO-Skills/skills/micro-writing-style) · ★1.4k · 2 copies)
- **microagent-create**: PROPOSE-ONLY — mine this host's recent Claude Code transcripts for work that keeps recurring and keeps returning bulk into context, rank those families by the context they burn, and write ONE draft mikro microagent into `.mikro/agents/&lt;name&gt;.proposed/` (agent.yaml + SYSTEM.md + EVIDENCE.md) for the user to approve. Read this skill when the user asks to create, propose, draft or suggest an mikro microagent, asks what is worth offloading, asks where their tokens are going and wants an agent for it, or runs /mikro:microagent-create. It never activates anything: `.proposed` is a reserved suffix t… ([automagik-si/genie](https://github.com/automagik-si/genie/tree/HEAD/mikro/plugins/claude-code/skills/microagent-create) · ★354 · 3 copies)
- **microapp-creator**: Scaffold, register, and dev-loop a new WibWob-DOS microapp end-to-end. Use when: "create a microapp", "new microapp", "scaffold a microapp", "add a microapp", "build a new app for wibwob", "make a wibwob app". ([j-greig/wibandwob-dos](https://github.com/j-greig/wibandwob-dos/tree/HEAD/.pi/skills/microapp-creator) · ★7)
- **microarch-analysis**: Use when analyzing microarchitectural attack surfaces by mapping shared hardware structures, identifying speculative execution vectors, quantifying speculative windows, and proposing countermeasures. Covers cache timing, transient execution, and contention channels. Do not use for RTL-level design review (use rtl-security-review) or physical implementation analysis (use physical-design-security). ([dtsong/my-claude-setup](https://github.com/dtsong/my-claude-setup/tree/HEAD/skills/council/forge/microarch-analysis) · ★6)
- **microarray-analysis**: Analyze DNA microarray data from GEO datasets. Use when asked to analyze microarray data, perform differential expression analysis, or when given a GEO accession (GSE*). Handles Affymetrix (CEL files) and Illumina (BeadChip) platforms with QC, normalization, statistical modeling, and gene ID mapping. ([gexijin/vitiligo](https://github.com/gexijin/vitiligo/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/microarray-analysis) · ★1)
- **microarray-background-subtraction**: Use when a quantile-normalized microarray intensity matrix (e.g. PIMENTo's preprocess.data.obj$quantile from a tab-separated file like example_unnorm.txt) shows a distinct low-intensity background peak in its log2 histogram that must be filtered out before downstream expression analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/microarray-background-subtraction))
- **microarray-batch-effect-removal**: Use when a normalized gene-level expression matrix aggregated across multiple GEO studies or platforms shows study-of-origin batch structure, and downstream comparison requires homogenized batch distributions while preserving perturbation/differentiation signal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/microarray-batch-effect-removal))
- **microarray-data-normalization**: Use when raw microarray intensity data (e.g., a tab-separated file like example_unnorm.txt with gene IDs, symbols, and per-sample intensity columns) must be normalized before differential expression or background subtraction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/microarray-data-normalization))
- **microarray-data-processing**: Use when a longitudinal peripheral-blood transcriptomics dataset needs per-patient time alignment to clinical nadir, and trajectories of candidate prognostic transcripts must be extracted and plotted across disease phases. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/microarray-data-processing))
- **microarray-dataset-preprocessing-validation**: Use when you have a gene-expression matrix (e.g. 17 DM2, 17 NGT, 9 IGT muscle profiles, or smoker/non-smoker airway epithelia data) and a set of predefined pathway gene lists (KEGG/Biocarta, ~400 pathways), and you need standardized per-gene expression plus a validated singular-value decomposition. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/microarray-dataset-preprocessing-validation))
- **microarray-differential-expression-analysis**: Use when you have a background-subtracted microarray expression matrix (e.g. Illumina Bead array data like E-MTAB-2400 WT vs villin-gp130Act crypt samples) and a class vector, and need significant genes plus sample-separation plots. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/microarray-differential-expression-analysis))
- **microarray-expression-data-loading**: Use when a transcriptomic regression/classification task needs a ready-made high-dimensional microarray expression matrix (p &gt;&gt; n, e.g. 6283 genes x 116 AML samples) packaged in the suffpcr R package rather than raw array files. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/microarray-expression-data-loading))
- **microarray-expression-data-loading**: Use when you have raw or normalized microarray expression matrices stored in public repositories (GEO accessions) along with sample metadata, and you need to load both into R as structured objects (expression matrix and phenotype data frame) to construct a design matrix and fit linear models for. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/microarray-expression-data-loading))
- **microarray-expression-data-processing**: Use when a two-group microarray comparison (e.g., current vs. never smokers, diabetic vs. non-diabetic) must be analyzed by ranking all genes by signal-to-noise and scoring ~400 predefined KEGG/Biocarta pathway gene-sets with permutation-based enrichment p-values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/microarray-expression-data-processing))
- **microarray-expression-data-retrieval**: Use when an analysis requires baseline (non-perturbation) microarray expression profiles for GDSC cancer cell lines, e.g. to score pathway-responsive gene signatures against IC50 drug sensitivity, starting from ArrayExpress/cancerrxgene accessions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/microarray-expression-data-retrieval))
- **microarray-expression-normalization**: Use when log-phase microarray expression data are available for paired strains that differ by a known duplication genotype, and the goal is to test whether genes within the duplicated region show induced expression relative to the non-duplicated clonal counterpart. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/microarray-expression-normalization))
- **microarray-expression-normalization-log-quantile**: Use when when you have raw or minimally processed microarray expression data (e.g., from GEO) with intensity values that exhibit sample-to-sample distributional differences and variance heterogeneity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/microarray-expression-normalization-log-quantile))
- **microarray-expression-standardization**: Use when a microarray expression matrix (e.g. muscle biopsies from DM2/NGT/IGT subjects or airway epithelia from smokers and non-smokers) must be gene-wise normalized to zero mean and unit variance across samples before translating gene expression levels into SVD-derived pathway 'activity' levels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/microarray-expression-standardization))
- **microarray-gene-expression-analysis**: Use when the goal is differential gene expression (e.g. knockdown-vs-control contrasts) on MDI-induced 3T3-L1 microarray samples with curated, normalized gene-level intensities available as a SummarizedExperiment object. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/microarray-gene-expression-analysis))
- **microarray-intensity-filtering**: Use when starting from a gene-level collapsed probe-intensity matrix (post-collapseRows) spanning multiple microarray studies, and a comparable normalized expression matrix is needed before batch-effect removal or downstream differential expression/gene set enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/microarray-intensity-filtering))
- **microarray-limma-moderated-statistics**: Use when the selected pdacR dataset's experiment type is Array (microarray) and a two-cohort A-vs-B differential expression comparison is requested via a sample track and comparison control. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/microarray-limma-moderated-statistics))
- **microarray-principal-component-analysis**: Use when working with curated microarray datasets (e.g. 3T3-L1 adipocyte series) where you need to check whether batch effects (data series, platform) dominate sample variance and whether a biological covariate like stage of differentiation separates samples after correction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/microarray-principal-component-analysis))
- **microarray-probe-gene-symbol-mapping**: Use when a downloaded GEO microarray series provides probe-level intensities with a probe-to-gene annotation table, and a gene-by-sample expression matrix (one row per unique gene symbol) is required for downstream analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/microarray-probe-gene-symbol-mapping))
- **microarray-probe-to-gene-annotation**: Use when input expression data are Affymetrix HG-U133 Plus 2.0 probe-level matrices (e.g., microarray CEL-derived probe IDs) that must be collapsed or mapped to gene symbols before downstream gene-name-based analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/microarray-probe-to-gene-annotation))
- **microarray-rnaseq-matrix-preprocessing**: Reference for cleaning raw gene-expression matrices (missing values, outliers, low-variance rows) before consensus partitioning or benchmarking clustering methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/microarray-rnaseq-matrix-preprocessing))
- **microarray-significance-output-gene-ranking**: Use when you have SAM significance output from samr testing on a microarray matrix and need tiered top-N gene lists plus a dendrogram heatmap to test whether samples cluster by phenotype/response class. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/microarray-significance-output-gene-ranking))
- **microarray-summarizedexperiment-data-access**: Use when a task requires gene expression data from MDI-induced differentiating 3T3-L1 pre-adipocytes under genetic (knockdown/overexpression) or pharmacological perturbation, and needs it loaded as a SummarizedExperiment object in R/Bioconductor. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/microarray-summarizedexperiment-data-access))
- **microbe**: Use when the user has a 16S or ITS feature table plus taxonomy and metadata and needs alpha/beta diversity, composition bars, differential abundance, co-occurrence networks, random-forest biomarkers, or a taxonomy tree. Drive the microbe MCP tools with local Rscript. ([MiniMax-AI/MiniMax-Code-Plugins](https://github.com/MiniMax-AI/MiniMax-Code-Plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/Presisitence/microbe/skills/microbe) · ★25)
- **microbe-compound-identification**: Use when you have collected MS/MS spectra from a microbial sample (pure culture, environmental isolate, or mixed community) and need to assign chemical identities to observed m/z features while simultaneously resolving which microbial taxa are likely producers of each metabolite. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/microbe-compound-identification))
